2026-10-19T03:29:25.2787244Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Build and serve the Firmus Prefill bundle: MegaMoE ping-pong, vectorized attention prologue with contiguous-KV FA3 extend, Prefill-only fused norm + quant

One build + serving run for every Prefill change found promising by the
probe job 39190318626 (research/runtime-performance/
2026-10-19-firmus-prefill-bottleneck-map.md §6), implemented before any
GPU use as the user asked. Baseline: main 7589c59 = 7999.37.

Contents (integration note:
research/runtime-performance/2026-10-19-firmus-prefill-bundle-integration.md):
- MegaMoE Prefill ping-pong (Codex, d9b1b2a): Prefill geometry 128x128,
  two consumer warpgroups, 5 stages, 168 registers, FP32 promotion of one
  warpgroup overlapping the other's WGMMA; host patch on top of the
  SwapAB-144 host patch, _C.so rebuilt in the image from
  patches/deep-gemm-host/series; header series 14 + SwapAB guard +
  ping-pong (pin 0c988075). Decode SASS unchanged. Basis: the standalone
  DeepGEMM grouped GEMM did the same work in 1.16 ms vs ~1.5 ms; expected
  Prefill MegaMoE 1.70 -> ~1.45 ms per call.
- Prefill attention (Codex, 2e85ef7): 16-byte-vectorized QK-norm/RoPE/
  FP8 kernel for EXTEND that also writes contiguous FP8 K/V, and FA3
  varlen on contiguous K/V for no-prefix EXTEND (probe: 399 -> 351 us).
  Decode TARGET_VERIFY keeps the paged path and its kernels.
- Fused input RMSNorm + QKV group quant only for EXTEND batches with at
  least 2048 rows (Decode regressed with it in 39119219065).
- Not included: flat NVLink barriers (a Prefill-only version cannot
  interoperate with the original barrier protocol when ranks run mixed
  geometries).
Runtime config unchanged from main (Decode cap 128, c192).

This commit itself adds gigatoken-encode.patch (already in the SGLang
chain since 2d294f0) to the image required-patch list and its test, a gap
noticed during integration.

Validation: host and header series apply to v0.1.5 and reproduce the
pin; 25 SGLang patches apply on fc275674 and all source checks pass;
Firmus-login CPU compile Prefill 168 regs / 0 spill, Decode and
Decode-16 unchanged; pytest, ruff, bash -n, tag check pass. First GPU
exercise of the ping-pong kernel and the host rebuild on this branch;
the pre-serving gate checks both before the server starts.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
